Abstract: High-resolution image restoration from degraded inputs is challenging because it must preserve global structural consistency while recovering fine-grained local details, especially at 4K resolution where direct diffusion-based restoration is computationally expensive and prone to repeated or inconsistent textures. In this work, we introduce MagnifiQ, an image restoration framework that progressively upscales and restores images across resolutions, e.g., from 1024x1024 to 4096x4096. Our approach leverages a pre-trained text-to-image diffusion model such as SDXL and adapts it for more scalable high-resolution inference by replacing its original self-attention layers with convolutional operations whose computational cost grows linearly with image resolution. We further propose a progressive upscaling strategy that iteratively restores images over multiple resolution stages, refining each intermediate output rather than directly hallucinating the final 4K image, thereby improving global coherence and reducing high-resolution artifacts. To enhance local details while controlling content drift, MagnifiQ uses patch-specific text prompts that provide spatially localized semantic guidance during restoration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>

*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of image editing models and their widespread application across various domains, there is an increasingly urgent need to deploy these model capabilities directly into real-world scenarios. However, existing benchmarks remain confined to simple single-image tasks, suffering from limited coverage dimensions and an inability to effectively differentiate performance among diverse models. Consequently, they fail to reliably evaluate model performance in complex multi-image editing, highly demanding reasoning instructions, and practical deployment settings. To address these limitations, we propose CPI-Bench, a Comprehensive, Practical andIntelligent benchmark for real-world image editing. CPI-Bench comprises three core subsets: CPI-General-Bench, which comprehensively covers diverse editing tasks and pioneers the inclusion of multi-image editing evaluation; CPI-Practical-Bench, which focuses on high-frequency real-user application scenarios; and CPI-Intelligent-Bench, which is dedicated to evaluating capabilities in highly demanding reasoning-based editing. Evaluation results of mainstream image editing models based on CPI-Bench demonstrate that CPI-Bench enhances performance differentiation among models. It provides a comprehensive and reliable quantification of gaps in general editing capabilities, practical deployment efficacy, and advanced reasoning-based editing, offering invaluable guidance for the future optimization of image editing models. Crucially, our ranking analysis reveals that CPI-Bench achieves the highest alignment with the Arena Image Edit Leaderboard, indicating it faithfully captures the preferences and perceptual judgments of human evaluators, serving as a robust proxy for real-world user experience.

</details>

### [68] [Does a Language Server Save Tokens for Coding Agents? A Measurement Methodology and Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13568)
*Pengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑"语义检索比词法检索更节省token"这一广泛假设。作者以tokens-to-success为指标，通过五臂消融实验（Python和TypeScript仓库，Claude Opus 4.8/Sonnet 4.6/Haiku 4.5）对比LSP语义检索与grep词法检索。结论是条件性的且通常是否定的：在符号定位任务上LSP多消耗6%-118%的token；在引用完整性任务上LSP提升精度但不省token且无法突破agent彻底性设定的召回上限；在多文件重命名这种需修改注释和字符串的真实编辑任务上，即使最完整的LSP也无法完全弥补与grep的差距，因为语义引用天然排除注释和字符串。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "语义检索更省token"的说法在编码agent领域被广泛主张却几乎无人实测——没有公开研究在同等任务成功率下隔离LSP与词法检索的token差异。作者旨在正式化这一问题，为agent检索策略提供有数据支撑的设计依据。

Method: 使用单一指标tokens-to-success；设计五臂消融实验以从混杂因素中隔离语义检索的效果；将三种事前声明的失败模式映射到可测量变量；在Python和TypeScript仓库上对Claude Opus 4.8、Sonnet 4.6、Haiku 4.5三个模型进行初步研究。

Result: 答案是条件性的且通常为否定：符号定位上LSP成本增加6%-118%，agent在免费时也忽略它；引用完整性上LSP提升精度但不省token，且只在最弱模型上才省token；真实测试执行评分下，grep完美解决多文件重命名，仅定位的LSP失败75%，即使完整、索引预热、文本丰富的LSP（每引用行内联）也只能恢复大部分差距但无法闭合。模型在定位任务默认用grep（语义使用率0-6%），而引用任务约半数自发启用LSP。

Conclusion: 结论不是"LSP永远好"或"永远坏"，而是需要依据任务类别、模型能力和词法噪声进行自适应路由的检索策略。语义检索在特定任务（引用完整性）有价值，但工具选择应视场景而定。

Abstract: Coding agents spend most of their context budget on retrieval. Lexical retrieval (grep) is universal, instant, and zero-setup, but noisy: it cannot tell a definition from a call from a comment. Semantic retrieval via the Language Server Protocol (LSP) is precise and typed, but needs a running, indexed server and pays a per-symbol round-trip. The claim that semantic retrieval is more token-efficient is, we find, asserted almost everywhere and measured almost nowhere: no public source isolates the LSP-vs-lexical token delta for an agent at equal task-success. This paper formalizes the question with one metric (tokens-to-success), specifies a five-arm ablation isolating semantic retrieval from confounds, maps three pre-stated failure modes onto measurable variables, and reports a preliminary study (Python and TypeScript repos; Claude Opus 4.8, Sonnet 4.6, Haiku 4.5). The answer is conditional and usually negative. On symbol-named localization the LSP costs tokens (+6% to +118%) and the agent ignores it when free. On reference-completeness it buys precision but not token savings and cannot raise the recall ceiling set by agent thoroughness; it saves tokens only for the weakest model. Tool choice is task-dependent: models default to grep on localization (0-6% semantic use) but reach for the LSP about half the time on reference tasks, unprompted. On edits scored by real test execution the gap is starkest: grep solves multi-file renames perfectly, a location-only LSP fails three-quarters of them by missing a call site, and even a complete, index-warmed, text-enriched LSP (each reference's line inline, as production LSP-MCP servers do) recovers most of the gap but cannot close it, since a rename must touch comments and strings that semantic references exclude. The implication is not LSP-always but an adaptive router keyed on task class, model capability, and lexical noise.

</details>


### [69] [Think in Latent, Explain in Language: Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13570)
*Dayuan Zhao,Shengcao Cao,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SELR框架，通过多任务训练目标（Answer Loss + CoT Loss）训练单一模型，使其既能进行高效的潜在推理（latent reasoning），又能将自身潜在表示解码为人类可理解的推理步骤，从而在无需外部解码器的情况下同时实现计算效率与内在可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理虽然相比文本CoT在计算效率上有显著优势，但其推理过程被压缩到潜在空间中变得不透明，难以解释。现有方法存在两难：要么是完全不可解释的"黑盒"（如Coconut），要么依赖分离的后置解码器（如Heima）来提供解释，这会引入架构开销并使解释与实际推理过程脱节。因此需要一种既能保持潜在推理高效性又能提供自包含可解释性的统一框架。

Method: 提出SELR（Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning）框架，核心是一个多任务训练目标，同时优化两个损失：(1) Answer Loss——优化潜在推理轨迹以产生准确的最终答案；(2) CoT Loss——显式训练同一模型将其自身潜在表示解码回人类可理解的推理步骤。这样生成的潜在表示既对任务有效又具有语义可解释性，无需外部解码器。

Result: 在LLM和VLM上均验证了SELR的有效性，相比基线方法，SELR在token效率和准确性上表现更优，且独特地提供了无需辅助模型的自我包含可解释性。

Conclusion: SELR成功打破了潜在推理中效率与可解释性之间的权衡，实现了单一模型同时具备高效潜在推理能力和内在自解释能力，消除了对外部解码器的依赖，为可解释潜在推理提供了统一解决方案。

Abstract: Latent reasoning has emerged as a powerful alternative to text-based Chain-of-Thought (CoT), offering significant gains in computational efficiency by compressing verbose reasoning into compact embeddings. However, compressing reasoning into the latent space renders the thinking opaque, hindering its interpretability. Current methods present a stark trade-off: they either function as unexplainable ''black boxes'' (e.g., Coconut), where the latent reasoning is not human-readable, or rely on separate post-hoc decoders for explainability (e.g., Heima), introducing architectural overhead and decoupling the explanation from the actual reasoning process. In this work, we present a unified framework for Self-Explainable Latent Reasoning (SELR) that trains a single model to perform efficient and inherently explainable latent reasoning. Our core contribution is a novel multi-task training objective that optimizes for two goals simultaneously: (1) an Answer Loss that optimizes the latent reasoning trajectory to produce accurate final answers, and (2) a CoT Loss that explicitly trains the same model to decode its own latent representations back into human-understandable reasoning steps. This design ensures that generated latent representations are both task-effective and semantically interpretable, eliminating the need for external decoders. We validate the effectiveness of SELR on both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), demonstrating that SELR achieves superior token efficiency and accuracy compared to baselines, while uniquely providing self-contained explainability without auxiliary models. Project page is available at https://jasondayuan.github.io/SELR/.

</details>


### [70] [Not All Tokens Are Equal: Inflation-Aware Routing for Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13571)
*Heming Fu,Shan Lin,Qianqian Xie,Guojun Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"token通胀"概念，指智能体系统因重试导致的真实成本与单次调用成本之间的差距，并设计了InflationAgent四阶段路由器来测量、预测并优化这一成本，在GSM8K上以更少token取得更高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由系统（如FrugalGPT）基于单次调用成本进行决策，忽略了智能体系统中失败重试带来的额外token消耗，从而严重低估真实工作流成本（困难任务上可低估超过2倍）。这导致成本优化决策失准。

Method: 提出InflationAgent四阶段路由器：(1) 系统化测量不同模型层级与任务类型下的token通胀，发现7B模型在多跳问答上通胀高达4.25倍；(2) 提出CoT Branching Entropy (CBE)，一种完全基于局部推理计算的预执行难度信号，用于预测高通胀（AUROC 0.887）；(3) 通过最大化语义汇率（SER）选择模型，SER = 期望准确率 / 预测真实成本，并采用fresh-escalation策略——在路由到更强模型前丢弃失败链。

Result: 在GSM8K固定预算下，InflationAgent达到94.7%准确率（FrugalGPT为91.0%），同时少用31%的token；将失败推理链转发给GPT-4o会使其准确率下降最高34.8个百分点，验证了fresh-escalation策略的必要性。

Conclusion: token通胀是智能体系统成本建模中不可忽视的因素，基于真实工作流成本而非单次调用成本进行路由决策能显著提升性价比；丢弃失败链的fresh-escalation策略优于直接复用失败推理链。

Abstract: When a language model fails to answer a query on the first attempt, an agentic system retries, consuming additional tokens each time. This retry overhead creates a gap between what a model's per-token price implies and what a full workflow actually costs. We call this gap \emph{token inflation} and define it as the ratio of true workflow cost to single-call cost. Systems like FrugalGPT route based on the latter, which can underestimate real cost by more than $2\times$ on difficult tasks. We address this with InflationAgent, a four-stage router that (1) measures token inflation systematically across model tiers and task types, finding inflation as high as $4.25\times$ for a 7B model on multi-hop question answering; (2) introduces CoT Branching Entropy (CBE), a pre-execution difficulty signal computed entirely from local inference, which predicts high inflation with AUROC 0.887; and (3) selects models by maximizing a Semantic Exchange Rate (SER) that divides expected accuracy by predicted true cost, with a fresh-escalation policy that discards failed chains before routing to a stronger model. On GSM8K under a fixed budget, InflationAgent achieves 94.7\% accuracy versus 91.0\% for FrugalGPT while using 31\% fewer tokens, and we show that forwarding a failed reasoning chain to GPT-4o reduces its accuracy by up to 34.8 percentage points, validating the fresh-escalation design.

</details>


### [71] [BCMT: Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13578)
*Rachid Arezki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BCMT（Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer），一种用于长上下文语言建模的架构。它通过在局部块内独立应用密集因果自注意力，并利用每个块的适应性摘要构建指数因果记忆来传播全局上下文信息，从而避免了传统Transformer的二次复杂度密集全局注意力与循环记忆架构中的学习记忆状态。在1024 token上下文的语言建模实验中，BCMT在验证性能上与Dense Transformer相当，同时显著提升了训练吞吐量并降低了内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的密集自注意力机制在序列长度上呈二次复杂度，限制了长上下文语言建模的效率；而现有的循环记忆架构依赖于学习到的记忆状态，且难以并行化。作者希望设计一种既避免密集全局注意力又无需学习记忆状态的架构，实现高效的长距离上下文传播。

Method: BCMT将序列划分为局部块，在每个块内独立应用密集因果自注意力处理局部token交互；每个块生成一个适应性摘要，通过这些摘要构建一个指数衰减的因果记忆（exponential causal memory），该记忆不包含可学习的记忆状态，完全基于块的摘要聚合而来；随后该记忆被注入回token表示中以传播长距离上下文。该记忆机制完全可并行化，与标准密集自注意力的实现兼容。

Result: 在上下文长度达1024 token的语言建模实验中，BCMT的验证性能与Dense Transformer相当，同时显著提升了训练吞吐量并降低了内存消耗。消融研究确认了这些性能提升来源于所提出的记忆机制。

Conclusion: 由块摘要构建的指数因果记忆可以作为密集全局注意力的有效替代方案，在保持模型性能的同时大幅提升计算与内存效率，为长上下文语言建模提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Transformer architectures rely on dense self-attention to model long-range dependencies, but this mechanism exhibits quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length. We introduce BCMT (Blockwise Causal Memory Transformer), an architecture for long-context language modeling that decouples local token interactions from global context propagation. Dense causal self-attention is applied independently within local blocks, while each block produces an adaptive summary aggregated through an exponential causal memory. This memory is subsequently injected back into the token representations, enabling efficient propagation of long-range contextual information without relying on explicit global attention. Unlike standard Transformers and recurrent memory architectures, BCMT maintains neither dense interactions between distant tokens nor learned memory states. Its memory mechanism is fully parallelizable and remains compatible with standard implementations of dense self-attention. Experiments on language modeling with context lengths of up to 1024 tokens show that BCMT achieves validation performance comparable to that of Dense Transformers while significantly improving training throughput and reducing memory consumption. An ablation study further confirms that these improvements arise from the proposed memory mechanism. These results demonstrate that an exponential causal memory constructed from block summaries provides an effective alternative to dense global attention mechanisms for long-context language modeling.

</details>

*Mohamed Anwar,Abed Alhakim Freihat,George Ibrahim,Mostafa Awad,Abdelrahman Sadallah,Gurpreet Gosal,Gokulakrishnan Ramakrishnan,Sarath Chandran,Biswajit Mishra,Rituraj Joshi,Ahmed Frikha,Etienne Goffinet,Abhishek Maiti,Ali El Filali,Sarah AlBarri,Samujjwal Ghosh,Rahul Pal,Parvez Mullah,Awantika Shukla,Sajid siddiki,Samta Kamboj,Onkar Pandit,Sunil Kumar Sahu,AbdelRahman Elbadawy,Amr Mohamed,Ahmad Chamma,Evan Dufraisse,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Dani Bouch,Hadi Abdine,Guokan Shang,Fajri Koto,Yuxia Wang,Zhuohan Xie,Ali Mekky,Rania Elbadry,Sarfraz Ahmad,Momina Ahsan,Omar El Herraoui,Daniil Orel,Hasan Iqbal,Kareem Elzeky,Mervat Abassy,Kareem Elozeiri,Saadeldine Eletter,Farah Atif,Nurdaulet Mukhituly,Haonan Li,Xudong Han,Aaryamonvikram Singh,Zainul Abedien Ahmed Quraishi,Neha Sengupta,Larry Murray,Avraham Sheinin,Joel Hestness,Natalia Vassilieva,Hector Xuguang Ren,Zhengzhong Liu,Michalis Vazirgiannis,Preslav Nakov*

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Jais 2，一个由MBZUAI、Cerebras和Inception联合开发的阿拉伯语为中心的大语言模型家族，包含最大规模从零训练的70B参数开源阿拉伯语模型以及具有竞争力的8B参数变体，在阿拉伯语及文化相关基准测试上表现优异，同时具备高计算效率和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动阿拉伯语中心大语言模型的发展，解决大规模阿拉伯语模型缺失的问题，同时兼顾语言多样性、文化保真度、开放性和推理速度。

Method: 采用自定义的阿拉伯语中心词表以提升训练和推理效率；通过优化的架构和训练策略实现高计算效率；使用比同类模型小得多的token预算进行训练；在Cerebras硬件上部署以支持高速推理。

Result: Jais 2在OALL2和AraGen基准上取得领先结果；在诗歌、宗教、美食、解梦等文化基准以及翻译、摘要等通用任务上表现强劲；70B模型推理速度高达每秒2000 tokens；模型以商业许可在HuggingFace上开源发布。

Conclusion: Jais 2通过融合规模、语言多样性、文化保真度、开放性和速度，为阿拉伯语中心LLM的研究和开发提供了一个开源权重基础，支持后续进一步研究与发展。

Abstract: Jais 2 is a family of Arabic-centric large language models developed jointly by MBZUAI, Cerebras, and Inception, designed to advance Arabic-centric language modeling, with strong performance across the Arabic and culturally grounded benchmarks evaluated in this report. The family includes, to our knowledge, the largest open Arabic-centric LLM trained from scratch at 70B parameters, and a competitive 8B-parameter variant among the evaluated open models. A custom Arabic-centric vocabulary enables efficient training and inference. In addition, an optimized architecture and training recipe yield highly compute-efficient training. With a substantially smaller token budget than comparable models, Jais 2 achieves strong Arabic performance on the benchmarks considered in this report and competitive English results. The models obtain leading results among the evaluated open models on OALL2 and AraGen. They also perform strongly on several culturally grounded Arabic benchmarks, including poetry, religion, cuisine, and dream interpretation, as well as in general tasks such as translation and summarization. We release the models in HuggingFace under a commercially permissive license. Jais 2 70B is also released as a chat app on the Web, iOS, and Android; it runs on Cerebras hardware, delivering up to 2,000 tokens per second, and enabling high-throughput Arabic-centric chat serving in our deployment setting. By uniting scale, linguistic diversity, cultural fidelity, openness, and speed, Jais 2 provides an open-weight foundation intended to support further research and development in Arabic-centric LLMs.

</details>


### [73] [IterCOMP: Reasoning-aware Adaptive Prompt Compression for Multi-hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13588)
*JungMin Yun,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IterCOMP是一个统一、无需训练的提示压缩框架，通过在迭代压缩循环中融入多跳推理，将文档分解为证据片段、评估问题可回答性并生成有针对性后续问题，逐步整合关键证据生成紧凑的推理导向提示。在MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA和HotpotQA上，该方法在减少token预算的同时显著提升了EM和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答需要跨多个证据片段进行复杂推理，而检索增强生成系统常因冗长且嘈杂的上下文被淹没，导致效率和准确率下降。现有提示压缩方法多针对单轮查询设计，无法捕捉相互依赖的推理步骤。

Method: IterCOMP采用迭代压缩循环：将文档分解为证据片段，评估问题可回答性，生成针对性后续问题，并逐步整合关键证据，最终构造紧凑的推理导向提示。该方法为统一架构且无需训练。

Result: 在MusiQue、2WikiMultiHopQA和HotpotQA三个基准数据集上，IterCOMP在Exact Match和F1分数上均取得显著提升，同时降低token开销，优于现有基线方法，并在推理复杂度增加时表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: IterCOMP通过将多跳推理融入迭代压缩过程，有效解决了现有提示压缩方法在多跳问答场景中的不足，实现了效率与准确率的双重提升，并具备随推理复杂度增加的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering requires complex reasoning across multiple evidence segments, which often overwhelms retrieval-augmented generation systems with lengthy and noisy contexts, thereby undermining both efficiency and accuracy. While existing prompt compression methods attempt to address this issue, they are typically designed for single-turn queries and fail to capture interdependent reasoning steps. We propose IterCOMP, a unified, training-free prompt compression framework that incorporates multi-hop reasoning within an iterative compression loop. IterCOMP decomposes documents into evidence segments, evaluates question answerability, and generates targeted follow-up questions to iteratively integrate essential evidence, producing a compact, reasoning-oriented prompt. Experiments on MusiQue, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and HotpotQA demonstrate that IterCOMP achieves substantial improvements in Exact Match and F1 scores while reducing the token budget, outperforming existing baselines and exhibiting robustness as reasoning complexity increases.

</details>


### [74] [Measuring Fairness in Large Audio Language Models via Semantic-Aware Bias Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13624)
*Zhe Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种语义感知的混合效应回归框架，用于评估大型音频语言模型在不同人口统计子群间的公平性，通过引入句子级语义嵌入作为协变量、将说话人身份建模为随机效应，有效控制了语义变化和说话人特征等混杂因素，减少了虚假的公平性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型音频语言模型在语音识别和音频问答等任务中广泛使用，但其跨人口统计子群的公平性问题日益受到关注。然而，口语输入场景下的公平性评估面临挑战，因为口语内容的语义变化和说话人特定特征等混杂因素可能被忽略，从而导致对模型偏见的误导性结论。

Method: 提出语义感知的混合效应回归框架，具体包括：(1) 将参考文本的句子级语义嵌入作为协变量纳入回归模型；(2) 将说话人身份建模为随机效应；(3) 语义表示直接从未评估的LALM自身提取，实现模型感知的语义变异控制。实验中在模拟数据和真实基准上进行了验证。

Result: 在模拟数据和真实世界基准上的实验表明，该方法显著减少了虚假的公平性发现，并能产生更稳健、更可解释的子群性能差异估计。

Conclusion: 语义感知的混合效应回归框架能够有效处理LALM公平性评估中的混杂因素，为音频模型公平性评估提供了更可靠的方法论基础。

Abstract: Large Audio Language Models (LALMs) have seen increasing use for audio understanding tasks such as speech recognition and audio question answering, raising concerns about fairness across demographic subgroups. Fairness evaluation in spoken-input settings is challenging due to confounding factors, including semantic variation in spoken content and speaker-specific characteristics. Ignoring these factors can result in misleading conclusions about model bias. We propose a semantic-aware mixed-effects regression framework for fairness evaluation in LALMs that explicitly accounts for these confounders. Our approach incorporates sentence-level semantic embeddings of reference text as covariates and models speaker identity as a random effect. Notably, semantic representations are extracted from the same LALM under evaluation, enabling semantic control over variation as perceived by the model itself. Experiments on simulated data and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed approach substantially reduces spurious fairness findings and yields more robust and interpretable estimates of subgroup performance differences.

</details>


### [75] [GRPO Beyond English: A Large-Scale Study of GRPO in Non-English and Multilingual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13698)
*Konstantin Dobler,Federico Scozzafava,Jonathan Janke,Mohamed Ali,Simon Lehnerer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型在多语言/非英语场景下的强化学习（RLVR+GRPO）进行了大规模实证研究，发现用母语训练推理与用英语训练效果差距很小，且存在显著的跨语言迁移效应，但具体趋势高度依赖模型和语言，某些语言训练会导致其他语言上的严重性能回退，因此需要广泛的多语言评估来检测退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR/GRPO相关研究高度以英语为中心，多语言和非英语场景下的强化学习推理行为缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 对多种基础模型、多种训练语言及不同推理语言奖励设置下的大规模GRPO实验，系统评估母语推理训练、跨语言迁移及潜在性能回退。

Result: 母语推理训练与英语推理训练的差距较小；跨语言迁移显著但具有模型和语言特异性；部分语言训练会引起其他语言领域能力严重回退。

Conclusion: 多语言RLVR能带来广泛跨语言收益，但必须辅以广泛的多语言评测以检测语言特定的性能退化。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), often optimized with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has become a central recipe for improving the reasoning capabilities of pretrained language models but current studies remain heavily English-centric. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of multilingual and non-English GRPO across a wide range of base models, training languages, and different reasoning language rewards. We find that training to reason in the native language often leaves only a small gap to training for English reasoning. We further observe strong crosslingual transfer: training in one language often improves performance in many others. However, specific trends are highly model- and language-dependent. In some cases, training in a particular language induces severe regressions on out-of-domain capabilities in other languages. Our analysis shows that RLVR beyond English can provide broad crosslingual gains, but also requires broad evaluation to detect language-specific regressions.

</details>


### [76] [CLAIR-Fin: An Adversarial Multi-Agent Framework for Claim-Level Verification and Adaptive Debate in Cross-Modal Financial QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13706)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,Jubayer Al Mahmud,M. F. Mridha,Md. Alam Hossain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLAIR-Fin，一个九智能体框架，通过类型化财务声明账本、不对称证据权威、链式保管验证、自适应反驳循环和持续幻觉风险指数等手段，在多智能体检索增强管线的各环节检测并缓解幻觉，在BB-FinQA-X跨模态财务评估集上显著提升了忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强和多智能体管线中的幻觉防御存在三点不足：证据在模态不一致时仍被信任；辩论仅验证汇总报告而非单个声明；验证仅发生在草稿完成之后，无法在最终文本生成前捕捉智能体间的错误。这些缺陷导致跨模态财务问答场景中的错误无法被及时且细粒度地检测。

Method: CLAIR-Fin包含九个智能体，核心机制包括：(1) 将每个问题分解为原子声明并维护在类型化的金融声明账本中；(2) 不对称证据权威机制，根据声明类型条件化地信任证据而非对所有模态一视同仁；(3) 链式保管验证，在草稿与对抗性审查之间的交接处检查grounding，而非仅在管线出口；(4) 自适应反驳循环，通过对抗性辩论路由争议声明，辩论深度随发现动态扩展；(5) 终端蕴含审计搭配持续幻觉风险指数，区分已通过审查与从未被质疑的声明，并在证据不足时弃答。

Result: 在BB-FinQA-X（包含500道基于孟加拉国银行年度报告的跨模态金融问题，按查询类型、格式和难度分层）上，相较于单次检索增强生成基线，忠实度从0.780提升至0.889；同时有5.4%的问题在证据不足时选择弃答而非强行生成无依据答案；相比HyDE和Graph-RAG等更强检索策略基线（忠实度≤0.874）也具有优势。

Conclusion: CLAIR-Fin通过将验证前移至各环节并细化到单个声明粒度，有效弥合了多智能体管线中幻觉防御的空白，证明了在跨模态金融场景中按声明类型差异化信任证据并结合持续风险监控能够显著提升输出忠实度。

Abstract: Existing defenses against hallucination in retrieval-augmented and multi-agent pipelines remain partial: evidence is trusted despite modality disagreement, debate verifies an aggregate report rather than individual claims, and such verification occurs only after drafting, leaving inter-agent errors undetected until the final text. To close this gap, we present CLAIR-Fin, a nine-agent framework that decomposes each question into atomic claims maintained in a typed Financial Claim Ledger. Each claim is resolved through Asymmetric Evidence Authority, which conditions evidence trust on claim type rather than treating all modalities as equally reliable; Chain-of-Custody Verification, which checks grounding at the hand-off between drafting and adversarial review rather than only at the pipeline's exit; an Adaptive Rebuttal Cycle, which routes contested claims through adversarial debate whose depth scales with what that debate finds; and a terminal entailment audit paired with a continuous Hallucination Risk Index that distinguishes claims that passed scrutiny from claims never contested. We evaluate CLAIR-Fin on BB-FinQA-X, a 500-question cross-modal financial evaluation set built from Bangladesh Bank Annual Report material, stratified by query type, format, and difficulty. Relative to a single-pass retrieval-augmented generation baseline, it raises faithfulness ($0.780 \rightarrow 0.889$) while abstaining on 5.4% of questions when evidence is insufficient rather than forcing an unsupported response, and it exceeds stronger retrieval-strategy baselines such as HyDE and Graph-RAG on faithfulness ($\leq 0.874$).

</details>


### [77] [TeachMateGPT: A Multi-Agent Knowledge-Grounded Framework for Pedagogical Assessment Generation from Science Curriculum Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13708)
*Fatema Tuj Johora Faria,Mukaffi Bin Moin,M. F. Mridha,Jubayer Al Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TeachMateGPT是一个面向课程对齐的科学评测题目自动生成的多智能体系统，通过层次化知识库(COPE)、分阶段故障关闭管线、源归因验证协议(SAVER)以及新的课程对齐数据集(NCTB-SciGen8)，在忠实度(0.68→0.96)和答案相关性(0.60→0.89)上显著超越传统RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索增强生成(RAG)的评测题目生成系统存在四大局限：扁平化检索缺乏层次结构、仅支持单一问题生成、缺乏对弱证据的防护机制、不适用于低资源且以统考(board-exam)为导向的课程体系。为减轻科学教师的工作负担，需要一种更可靠、结构化的自动题目生成方案。

Method: 提出TeachMateGPT多智能体系统，包含四项核心贡献：(i) COPE层次化知识库，以多分辨率索引沿教学大纲结构分割文档，并通过可遍历的图式谱系在三个粒度级别链接文档；结合证据与各主题的教学层级。(ii) 分阶段故障关闭智能体管线，以路由门控搜索，融合稠密与词法证据，在覆盖度门控下对证据不足时拒绝生成，由专家智能体分别起草客观题和构答式开放题。(iii) SAVER源归因验证协议，依据检索证据对忠实度、相关性和幻觉风险进行评分，对每个创造性问题的四个子部分实施更严格的接地检查，并与教师参与的评估相结合而非自动过滤。(iv) NCTB-SciGen8数据集，涵盖NCTB八年级科学教科书全部14章，包含198道题目(143道选择题、55道创造性问题)，由该管线生成并经三位在职教师评分。

Result: 与vanilla RAG基线相比，TeachMateGPT将忠实度从0.68提升至0.96，答案相关性从0.60提升至0.89，在评测题目生成的可靠性和课程对齐质量上均取得显著改善。

Conclusion: TeachMateGPT通过多智能体架构、层次化知识检索和源归因验证机制，有效解决了课程语料上自动评测题目生成的质量问题，为低资源、统考结构化的科学课程提供了可行的自动化评测编写方案，同时引入教师循环评估以确保教育质量。

Abstract: Automatically generating textbook-grounded assessment items can reduce science teachers' workload, but existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems rely on flat retrieval, support only single-question generation, lack safeguards against weak evidence, and are ill-suited to low-resource, board-exam-structured curricula. We address these limitations with TeachMateGPT, a multi-agent system contributing four advances to curriculum-grounded science-assessment authoring. (i) COPE, a hierarchical knowledge base replacing token-window chunking with a multi-resolution index that segments documents along syllabus structure and links them at three granularities via a traversable graph-based lineage, matching evidence to each topic's instructional level. (ii) A staged, fail-closed agent pipeline replacing one-shot retrieve-then-generate: routing gates search, retrieval fuses dense and lexical evidence under a coverage gate that withholds generation on insufficient evidence, and specialist agents draft objective and constructed-response items. (iii) SAVER, a source-attributed verification protocol scoring faithfulness, relevance, and hallucination risk against retrieved evidence, applying stricter grounding checks across each creative question's four sub-parts, paired with teacher-in-the-loop evaluation rather than automatic filtering. (iv) NCTB-SciGen8, a curriculum-grounded dataset of 198 items (143 multiple-choice, 55 creative questions) spanning all 14 chapters of the NCTB Class 8 science textbook, produced by the pipeline and rated by three practicing teachers. TeachMateGPT raises faithfulness (0.68 $\rightarrow$ 0.96) and answer relevancy (0.60 $\rightarrow$ 0.89) over a vanilla RAG baseline.

</details>


### [78] [StreamHear: Domain-Adapted Pseudo-Labeling for Semi-Supervised Streaming Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13717)
*Zefang Liu,Chenyang Zhu,Sangwoo Cho,Xujun Peng,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamHear是一个半监督pipeline，通过离线transducer教师模型在标记数据上微调、对无标记数据生成伪标签，再混合微调流式学生模型，并引入先验正则化的动态规划重对齐步骤来修正chunk级词位置，从而在域偏移场景下提升流式ASR性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式自动语音识别（ASR）在域偏移的目标音频上性能不佳，而获取域内标注数据成本高昂，同时无标注音频大量可得。需要一种利用无标注数据来提升流式ASR对域偏移鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 提出StreamHear半监督pipeline：(1) 在带标签训练集上微调离线transducer教师模型；(2) 用教师模型对无标签音频生成伪标签；(3) 在混合数据上微调预训练的流式学生模型；(4) 引入先验正则化的动态规划（prior-regularized dynamic programming）重对齐步骤，利用ASR假设锚点修正chunk级词位置。

Result: 在四个跨越金融电话、准备性朗读语音和手机质量对话的数据集上，StreamHear一致优于有监督的学生微调方法，并缩小了与离线教师模型之间的性能差距。

Conclusion: StreamHear证明了半监督teacher-student框架结合伪标签与重对齐技术，能够有效利用充足的无标注域内音频来提升流式ASR在域偏移场景下的性能，且无需昂贵的域内标注数据。

Abstract: Streaming automatic speech recognition (ASR) underperforms on domain-shifted target audio, where labeled in-domain data is costly to prepare while unlabeled audio is abundant. We present StreamHear, a semi-supervised pipeline that adapts a pretrained streaming student by fine-tuning an offline transducer teacher on the labeled training set, generating pseudo-labels on the unlabeled portion, and fine-tuning the student on the mixture. We further introduce a prior-regularized dynamic-programming realignment step that fixes chunk-level word placement using an ASR-hypothesis anchor. Across four datasets spanning financial calls, prepared read speech, and phone-quality dialogue, StreamHear consistently outperforms supervised student fine-tuning and narrows the gap to the offline teacher.

</details>


### [79] [BM25-Augmented Many-Shot Translation for Low-Resource North-Eastern Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13722)
*Aashish Dhawan,Christopher Driggers-Ellis,Dzmitry Kasinets,Christan Grant,Daisy Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了佛罗里达大学Gators团队参加WMT26低资源印度语言翻译共享任务的系统。该系统采用检索增强的多示例翻译管线，在推理时通过BM25从语言专属训练库中检索最相似的平行例句，并利用Gemini 2.5 Flash基于这些示例进行翻译，无需微调模型。训练库结合了WMT26官方数据与Samanantar等公开语料。通过网格搜索为所有22个语言方向对选择最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源印度语言（特别是东北印度语言）的机器翻译面临数据稀缺挑战，作者旨在利用检索增强的多示例学习方法在无需模型微调的情况下提升翻译质量，并系统性地探索最优的检索与示例配置。

Method: 采用检索增强的多示例翻译管线：推理时使用BM25从语言专属训练库中检索最相似的平行示例，将这些示例作为上下文条件输入给Gemini 2.5 Flash进行翻译。训练库由WMT26官方数据与Samanantar等公开语料（含先前WMT共享任务数据）合并构建。对所有22个英语-印度语言方向对，通过网格搜索检索数量r和开发集示例数量d来确定每个提交的最佳配置。

Result: 系统支持英语与十一种东北印度语言之间的双向翻译，覆盖22个语言方向对，每个方向对均通过网格搜索确定了最优的检索与示例配置。

Conclusion: 检索增强的多示例翻译管线（无需微调）可以有效应用于低资源印度语言翻译，通过数据增强和系统化的参数选择能够为每个语言方向优化翻译性能。

Abstract: This paper describes the University of Florida Gators submission to the WMT26 Low-Resource Indic Language Translation shared task. We adapt the retrieval-augmented many-shot translation pipeline from our AmericasNLP 2026 system to translate between English and eleven North-Eastern Indian languages in both directions. At inference time, BM25 retrieves the most similar parallel examples from a language-specific training bank, and Gemini 2.5 Flash translates the input conditioned on these examples. No model fine-tuning is involved. Training banks combine official WMT26 data with publicly available corpora such as Samanantar and prior WMT shared task releases. A grid search over retrieval count r and development exemplar count d across all 22 language-direction pairs selects the best configuration for each submission.

</details>


### [80] [GALA: Generation-Aware Cross-Modal Alignment for Text-to-Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13741)
*Haochen Zhang,Gengwei Zhang,Laura Yao,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GALA，一种通过跨模态对齐来实现文本条件时间序列生成的两阶段方法。GALA首先利用对比学习和辅助生成损失，将预训练文本编码器与时间序列基础模型耦合到共享嵌入空间，然后冻结生成的标题嵌入来驱动流匹配生成器。在TSFragment-600K基准上，GALA在36个指标列中30个排名第一，并打破了生成器内部文本编码器所导致的保真度与标题符合度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本条件时间序列生成器要么使用冻结的现成文本编码器嵌入，要么端到端适应编码器但仅将去噪损失作为副产物来塑造嵌入。这两种方式都未能有意地将条件表示与信号模态匹配，导致条件表示不适合指导生成过程。

Method: GALA采用两阶段方法：第一阶段，通过对比学习将预训练文本编码器与时间序列基础模型耦合到共享嵌入空间，同时使用辅助生成损失使两个编码器都适应生成任务；第二阶段，冻结生成的标题嵌入以驱动流匹配生成器。该方法还通过消融实验验证了辅助生成损失在对齐中的必要性。

Result: 在TSFragment-600K数据集上（涵盖四个领域和三种片段长度），GALA在36个指标列中30个排名第一，在长度24/48/96下平均排名为1.08/1.08/1.42，而最强基线为1.92/2.00/1.75。相比生成器内部文本编码器存在的保真度与标题符合度之间的权衡，GALA同时提升了FID、CTTP和JFTSD。消融辅助损失会导致这三个指标同时退化。

Conclusion: GALA证明将条件表示与信号模态进行生成感知的跨模态对齐，能够打破保真度与文本符合度之间的权衡，显著提升文本条件时间序列生成质量。辅助生成损失是对齐方案中不可分割的必要组成部分。

Abstract: Synthesizing time series from natural language is emerging as the most expressive form of controllable time series generation. However, existing text-conditioned generators either take caption embeddings frozen from off-the-shelf text encoders, or adapt the encoder end-to-end, letting the denoising loss shape the embeddings only as a by-product. In either case, the conditioning representation is never deliberately matched to the signal modality, leaving it ill-suited to guide generation. We address this by introducing GALA: Generation-Aware cross-modaL Alignment for text conditional time series generation. GALA is a two-stage approach that first contrastively couples a pretrained text encoder with a time-series foundation model into a shared embedding space with both encoders adapted to generation by an auxiliary generative loss, and then freezes the resulting caption embedding to drive a flow-matching generator. On TSFragment-600K, spanning four domains and three fragment lengths, GALA sets a new state of the art, ranking first in 30 of 36 metric columns and reaching an average rank of 1.08/1.08/1.42 at lengths 24/48/96 against 1.92/2.00/1.75 for the strongest baseline. We further find that generator-internal text encoders force a trade-off between fidelity and caption adherence, whereas conditioning on the aligned embedding breaks it: FID, CTTP, and JFTSD all improve at once. Ablating the auxiliary loss degrades FID, CTTP and JFTSD together, it indicates the generative term is a necessary component of the alignment rather than an add-on.

</details>


### [81] [Amplified Does Not Mean Predictive: Reasoning Behaviors in Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13760)
*Jean de Dieu Nyandwi,Leena Mathur,Yonatan Bisk,Robert Hawkins,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Behavioral Lift指标，量化推理模型中特定行为与答案正确性的关联强度。通过对15个模型、6个基准、15,282条推理轨迹的分析，发现存在"放大-提升差距"（Amplification-Lift Gap）：推理导向训练显著放大了自我纠正、假设检验和不确定性表达等行为，但这些行为与正确性的关联并不强；而置信度校准、知识对齐和自我意识等与正确性高度相关的行为却几乎未被放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理导向训练可能让推理轨迹看起来更像深思熟虑（deliberative），但没有真正放大与模型正确性最相关的行为。理解"哪些行为真正关联正确答案"与"训练实际放大了哪些行为"之间的差异，对于改进推理模型训练至关重要。

Method: 引入Behavioral Lift指标，度量某一行为在推理轨迹中出现与不出现时正确答案率的变化。构建一个同时适用于LLM和VLM轨迹的分类体系，涵盖核心行为类别。在15个模型、6个基准（涵盖纯文本和视觉-语言推理）上标注了15,282条推理轨迹并进行统计分析。

Result: 发现Amplification-Lift Gap：思考模型强烈放大了自我纠正、假设检验和不确定性表达（不确定性表达放大3-7倍），但最高的Lift行为是置信度校准、知识对齐和自我意识。置信度校准在两种模态中都是正确性的最强正向信号之一，却几乎没有被放大；不确定性表达虽被放大3-7倍，却与正确性关联很弱甚至为负。

Conclusion: 推理导向训练并未优先放大具有最高Lift的行为，这提示需要过程级（process-level）的训练目标来奖励校准且基于事实的推理，而非仅奖励表面的推理形式。

Abstract: Which reasoning behaviors are associated with correct answers in reasoning models, and does reasoning-oriented training amplify those behaviors? This distinction is important because reasoning-oriented training can make traces look more deliberative without amplifying the behaviors most tied to model correctness. We quantify this mismatch with Behavioral Lift, a metric that measures how much correctness changes when a behavior is present versus absent in a model's reasoning trace. Across 15 models and 6 benchmarks spanning text-only and vision-language reasoning, we annotate 15,282 traces with a taxonomy whose core behaviors are defined for both LLM and VLM traces. We find evidence for an Amplification-Lift Gap, in which thinking models strongly amplify self-correction, hypothesis testing, and uncertainty acknowledgment, while the highest-lift behaviors are confidence calibration, knowledge alignment, and self-awareness. Confidence calibration is among the strongest positive signals of correctness in both modalities, yet is barely amplified; uncertainty acknowledgment is amplified by 3--7$\times$, yet is weakly or negatively associated with correctness. We find that reasoning-oriented training does not preferentially amplify the highest-Lift behaviors, motivating process-level objectives that reward calibrated and grounded reasoning rather than surface form alone.

</details>


### [82] [When Lexical Change Misleads: Rethinking Dynamic Topic Model Evaluation with Traditional and LLM-Based Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13835)
*Charu Karakkaparambil James*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估动态主题模型（CoNTM和DLDA）在三个数据集上的主题质量，发现传统时间一致性度量与人类判断相关性波动大（ρ=-0.256到0.614），而基于LLM的语义相似度在CoNTM上与人类语义判断高度一致（ρ=0.502-0.721），但对DLDA一致性较差。词彙变化分层揭示了聚合评估掩盖的差异，因此建议采用词彙变化感知的评估方式，将传统一致性与LLM语义度量作为互补信号联合报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态主题模型中的词分布随时间演化，但词汇变化时传统一致性度量可能失效（语义保持不变而词汇变化）。现有评估方法未考虑词彙变化分层对评估结果的影响，需要更合适的评估方法。

Method: 使用CoNTM和DLDA两个动态主题模型，在NYT、DBLP和arXiv三个数据集上评估120个主题，由三位人类标注者对主题进行词彙变化分层（Low、Medium、High三个类别），比较传统时间一致性与LLM语义相似度两种度量与人类判断的相关性。

Result: 传统时间一致性与人类判断的相关性极不稳定（ρ=-0.256到0.614）；LLM语义相似度在CoNTM上与人类语义判断强一致（NYT:0.609, DBLP:0.721, arXiv:0.502），但对DLDA一致性较差。词彙变化分层揭示了聚合评估中隐藏的差异。

Conclusion: 应在动态主题模型评估中采用词彙变化感知（lexical-change-aware）的评估策略，将传统时间一致性与LLM语义度量作为互补、不可互换的信号联合报告，而非依赖单一度量。

Abstract: Dynamic topic models capture evolving word distributions, but traditional coherence metrics may fail when vocabulary changes while semantic meaning persists. We evaluate 120 topics from CoNTM and DLDA across NYT, DBLP, and arXiv, using three human annotators and Low, Medium, and High lexical-change categories. Traditional temporal coherence shows highly variable agreement with human judgments ($ρ$=-0.256 to 0.614). In contrast, LLM-based semantic similarity agrees strongly with human semantic judgments for CoNTM on NYT ($ρ$=0.609), DBLP ($ρ$=0.721), and arXiv ($ρ$=0.502), but is less consistent for DLDA. Lexical-change stratification reveals variation hidden by aggregate evaluation. We therefore advocate lexical-change-aware evaluation, jointly reporting traditional coherence and LLM-based semantic measures as complementary rather than interchangeable signals.

</details>


### [83] [ASSERT: A Measurement Pipeline for GenAI Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13840)
*Riccardo Fogliato,Abhinav Palia,Xiawei Wang,Emily Sheng,Chad Atalla,Jean Garcia-Gathright,Nicholas Pangakis,Sharman Tan,Dan Vann,Hannah Washington,P. Alex Dow,Heba Elfardy,Hanna Wallach,Sandeep Atluri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASSERT，一个面向生成式AI审计的规范驱动测量流程，将每个报告率与生成该率的具体测量选择规范绑定，以提高审计结果的透明度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统的审计通常将行为总结为单一报告率，用于比较系统、追踪回退和门控部署。然而报告率同时反映被审计系统和背后的测量选择，当率变化时难以判断是系统变化还是测量选择变化所致，导致归因模糊、比较失真。

Method: ASSERT采用规范驱动的测量流程：先协助起草行为评分标准和测试用例，形成书面的测量选择规范，然后对GenAI系统运行审计并输出报告率。通过将每个报告率绑定到明确的规范文档，使跨审计差异可归因、可解释。

Result: 在对话欺骗的案例研究中，报告率随对话设定、模拟用户、评判者和不合规证据门槛的变动而显著变化，这些测量选择不仅大幅改变报告率，还会重排GenAI系统的排名顺序。

Conclusion: 由于每个报告率都绑定显式规范，审计之间的差异更容易归因和解读，ASSERT提升了GenAI审计结果的可重复性和可比性，为审计社区提供了一种提高测量透明度的框架。

Abstract: Audits of generative AI (GenAI) systems often summarize behavior as a reported rate: how often the audited system complies with policy. Researchers and stakeholders use that rate to compare systems, track regressions, and gate deployment. A reported rate reflects both the system under audit and the measurement choices behind it, so a change in the rate can leave it unclear whether the system or those choices moved. We introduce ASSERT, a specification-driven measurement pipeline for GenAI audits that ties each reported rate to a written specification of the measurement choices used to produce it. ASSERT helps draft a behavioral rubric and test cases, then runs the audit against a GenAI system and returns a reported rate. In a case study on conversational deception, we observe that the reported rate moves substantially with the dialogue setup, the simulated user, the judge, and the evidence bar for non-compliance. These measurement choices substantially change the reported rate and can reorder GenAI system rankings. Because each reported rate is tied to an explicit specification, differences across audits are easier to attribute and interpret.

</details>


### [84] [Bootstrapping Niche Multilingual Code Translation via Reinforcement Learning with Execution-Based Verifiable Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13854)
*Kouki Yuki,Jie Zeng,Kyoko Ogawa,Ryunosuke Ikeda,Yohei Kobashi,Takeshi Kojima,Ikuya Yamada,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于执行监督和偏好强化学习（GRPO）的多语言代码翻译方法，解决了小众语言对之间并行监督稀疏导致翻译不可执行的问题。通过扩展现有Python种子程序生成多语言执行验证的代码池，训练奖励模型，并对600个（25×24）语言对进行偏好优化。作者引入HumanEval-X++执行基准评估多对多翻译能力，4B模型在HumanEval-X++上平均提升13%，中端语言提升21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经代码翻译主要集中在C++、Java、Python等少数流行语言上，忽略了多对多（many-to-many）语言翻译场景。在稀疏语言对场景下，由于缺乏并行监督数据，现有模型生成的翻译虽然表面可行但无法执行，需要一种可靠的训练和评估方法来提升多语言翻译的可执行性。

Method: 1) 数据生成：将已验证的Python种子程序扩展为多语言池，每个代码经过执行验证。2) 训练数据构建：基础LLM在语言对之间生成翻译候选，根据执行结果标注偏好。3) 奖励模型：使用偏好数据训练奖励模型来评分跨语言翻译质量。4) 策略优化：使用GRPO在600个语言对上优化基础LLM，以奖励模型为训练信号。5) 评估基准：引入HumanEval-X++执行基准，将HumanEval-X扩展至广泛的多对多语言空间。

Result: 使用Qwen-3.5 4B和9B模型进行评估，在HumanEval-X++和现有基准上均比未训练基线有持续提升。4B模型在HumanEval-X++上所有语言平均提升13%，中端语言上提升达21%。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一套可靠的数据生成、训练和基准测试流程，为多对多编程语言翻译质量的进一步自举提升铺平了道路，验证了基于执行监督的偏好强化学习在稀疏语言对场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Code translation must preserve executable behavior across many programming languages, yet neural code translation has largely focused on a few popular languages such as C++, Java, and Python. This leaves a niche, many-to-many setting where parallel supervision is sparse, producing plausible but non-executable translations. We address this setting with preference-based reinforcement learning driven by execution-based supervision. Our pipeline firstly expands verifiable seed Python programs into a multilingual pool of execution-validated codes. Using the pool, a base LLM generates translation candidates across language pairs, which we label by their execution outcomes. The resulting preferences are used to train a reward model that scores cross-language translation quality. Finally, we optimize our base LLMs with GRPO over 600 directed language pairs (25 x 24) using the reward model as a signal. To evaluate the niche translation capability, we introduce HumanEval-X++, an execution-based benchmark that extends HumanEval-X to a broad many-to-many language space. We evaluate our approach using Qwen-3.5 4B and 9B models. On HumanEval-X++ and existing benchmarks, it yields consistent gains over the untrained baselines. In particular, the 4B model achieves an average improvement of 13% across all languages on HumanEval-X++, with a gain of 21% on mid-tier languages. Our study establishes a reliable approach of data generation, training, and benchmarking, paving the way toward further bootstrapping the quality of many-to-many translation for programming languages.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling Creative Writing Beyond Story-Centric Data with Attribute-Guided Genre Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13947)
*Hwan Chang,Yongil Kim,Heuiyeen Yeen,Yireun Kim,Jinsik Lee,Hwanhee Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种属性引导的体裁扩展框架，用于将LLM的创意写作数据从纯故事类扩展至多元体裁。通过将主题广度与体裁形式控制分离，利用人类编写的故事提示作为创意种子，结合手工策划的体裁属性生成符合体裁规范的查询-响应对，构建了包含13种创意体裁、5万样本的Multi-Genre Collection语料库。实验表明，基于该数据微调的模型在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上均优于基座模型、写作专用基线和现有写作语料训练的模型，且控制性体裁扩展比单纯的故事中心扩展更能驱动强大的创意写作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量创意写作数据长期被以故事为中心的数据主导，而LLM需要学习和遵循不同创意格式的结构性与功能性惯例（如歌词、剧本、游戏设计等），现有数据无法支撑模型在多样化创意体裁上的跟随能力。

Method: 提出属性引导的体裁扩展框架：将主题广度与体裁形式控制解耦，以人类编写的故事提示作为多样化创意种子，结合手工策划的体裁属性（结构、风格、格式惯例）来提示强LLM生成体裁忠实的查询-响应对，随后进行质量过滤。

Result: 构建了Multi-Genre Collection语料库（50K样本，13种创意体裁）。在分布外写作基准和保留体裁诊断上，微调模型一致超越基座模型、写作专用基线和现有写作语料训练的模型。体裁数量消融实验表明，受控的体裁扩展（而非仅故事中心扩展）是提升创意写作能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了属性引导的体裁扩展能够有效提升LLM的跨体裁创意写作能力，且体裁多样性与控制性的平衡比单纯扩大故事数据量更具价值，为创意写作数据的规模化生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: High-quality creative writing data for large language models (LLMs) remains dominated by story-centric data, limiting models' ability to follow the structural and functional conventions of diverse creative formats. We propose an attribute-guided genre expansion framework for scaling creative writing data beyond story generation. By separating thematic breadth from genre-form control, our framework leverages human-authored story prompts as diverse creative seeds, while utilizing manually curated genre attributes to enforce distinct structural, stylistic, and formatting conventions. We combine these to prompt strong LLMs for genre-faithful query-response pairs, which are then quality-filtered. Applying this framework, we construct the Multi-Genre Collection, a 50K-example corpus spanning 13 creative genres, including story, rap, lyrics, scripts, game design, character design, and other creative formats. Experiments across out-of-distribution writing benchmarks and held-out genre diagnostics demonstrate that models fine-tuned on our data consistently surpass not only base models and writing-specialized baselines, but also models trained on existing writing corpora. Genre-count ablations further indicate that controlled genre expansion, rather than story-centric scaling alone, is a key driver of robust creative writing capability.

</details>


### [86] [Repair, Not Improvement: Decomposing Constrained Decoding in Tool-Call Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13959)
*Janghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察了约束生成（constrained generation）方法在函数调用（function calling）场景中的表现，特别聚焦于"工具弃权"（tool abstention）这一被原研究明确排除的情况。实验通过在相同提示下分离语法的两个功能（决定生成停止位置与决定可输出token），在0.6B至4B的开源模型上对英文和韩文进行配对比较。结果显示：在弃权任务上，先前工作的约束生成方法在六个单元格中有四个出现显著的负面效果（最差达-29.5个百分点），而在任何单元格中都没有显著正面效果。虽然枚举（enum）约束在恢复答案形式上有所帮助（698个修复中545个原本没有可读答案），但在需要调用工具的项目上整体效果始终为正面。两个预先注册的语言假设均未得到支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近关于约束生成的研究明确将函数调用排除在其理论框架之外，因为该框架假设约束仅涉及格式而不编码正确性要求。工具弃权（模型决定不调用任何工具）是对此最尖锐的检验场景，因为枚举约束不会改变答案的措辞，只会缩小答案集合，而"不调用"是首先被丢弃的选项。作者旨在测量这个被排除的案例，从而检验约束生成方法在函数调用这一安全性关键场景中的真实效果。

Method: 设计三组条件，在字节相同的提示上分离语法的两项功能：(1) 决定生成停止位置（stop token），(2) 决定哪些token允许发射（enum）。使用0.6B至4B的开源模型，在匹配的英文和韩文项目上进行评估，使语言比较在项目内完成。弃权（abstention）作为预先注册的主要度量指标，并同时报告池化（pooled）结果。

Result: 在弃权任务上，约束生成相对无约束解码器在六个单元格中有四个呈现负面效果，区间排除零，最差达-29.5个百分点；没有任何单元格呈现排除零的正面效果。总效果是正负符号相反的加和：在最小模型韩文上，stop token带来-20.0的代价，enum带来+19.5的收益，两者合计-0.5。被修复的698个弃权案例中，545个原本没有可读答案，0个是评分者拒绝判断的。在需要工具的项目上，效果全程为正面。两个预先注册的语言假设均失败。

Conclusion: 约束生成在函数调用/工具弃权场景中的作用并非一致正面：其"停止位置"约束可能带来显著性能代价，而token范围约束（enum）则有助于恢复答案的形式。总体效果取决于模型与语言的组合，且无法保证正面收益。需要工具的具体项目上约束生成有效，但以弃权度量的整体效果在语言间和模型间具有高度异质性。预先注册的语言假设均不被数据支持，表明对该效应的语言相关预测需要重新审视。

Abstract: Function calling is what the recent accounting of constrained generation explicitly sets aside: it finds the decoder's contribution small for format constraints, then warns in its Section 7 against extrapolating where a constraint encodes a correctness requirement, and names function calling as one. Tool abstention is that case at its sharpest: an enum leaves the wording of an answer alone and narrows the set of answers there are, and declining to call anything is the first it drops. We measure the excluded case. Three conditions over one byte-identical prompt separate a grammar's two jobs: it fixes where generation stops as well as which tokens may be emitted. We evaluate open-weight models from 0.6B to 4B on matched English and Korean items, so the language comparison is made within item. Against an unconstrained decoder, prior work's contrast is negative on abstention in four of six cells with intervals excluding zero, worst -29.5 points, and positive with an interval excluding zero in none. The total is a sum with opposite signs: on the smallest model in Korean the stop token costs -20.0, the enum returns +19.5, and the two leave -0.5. What it recovers is form: of 698 abstentions repaired, 545 had no readable answer and 0 were judgements the scorer refused. On tool-needed items it is positive throughout; abstention leads because it is the preregistered measure, and the pooled number being kinder to the intervention makes moving to it worse rather than better. Both preregistered language claims fail.

</details>


### [87] [Batch-wise Adaptive Pruning: Periodic Neuron Activation-Aware Weight Pruning for Language Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14003)
*Yongmin Kim,Shota Takashiro,Yusuke Iwasawa,Takeshi Kojima,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专为大规模推理模型（LRMs）批量推理设计的免训练自适应剪枝方法，通过周期性top-k选择替代阈值选择，并引入激活记忆机制累积重要神经元，解决了批量推理下剪枝阈值失配导致精度崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRMs通过长链推理获得强性能但推理成本高昂。现有免训练自适应剪枝方法在批量推理场景下性能严重退化，因为批量推理需共享单一剪枝掩码，激活聚合后阈值不再匹配，导致稀疏率漂移和推理任务精度崩溃。

Method: 方法包含两个核心组件：(1) 用周期性top-k选择替代逐token的阈值选择，在聚合的重要性分数上运行，不受聚合导致的激活分布偏移影响，且每个更新周期仅执行一次选择以保持加速；(2) 基于重要神经元在长推理过程中周期性复燃的观察，引入激活记忆机制，跨更新阶段累积重要性分数，从而保留周期性出现的神经元。

Result: 在多样化的推理基准上，所提方法在batch size为4、50%目标稀疏度下，平均准确率比最先进的免训练自适应剪枝方法高39.7个百分点（DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B），并在50%实际稀疏度下相比稠密推理达到1.40倍加速。

Conclusion: 针对批量推理设计的训练免自适应剪枝方法有效解决了聚合激活分布偏移导致的精度崩溃问题，显著提升了推理效率与准确率的平衡，为LRMs在生产环境中的高吞吐量部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on complex tasks through extended chain-of-thought generation, but incur substantial computational costs during inference. In production settings, batched inference is essential for high throughput, yet the existing training-free adaptive pruning methods we evaluate severely degrade in this regime. Because a batch must share a single pruning mask, these methods aggregate activations across samples and then apply threshold-based selection; the threshold, calibrated offline on unaggregated activations, no longer matches the aggregated distribution, so the realized sparsity ratio drifts and accuracy on reasoning tasks collapses under batched inference.
  In this work, we propose a training-free adaptive pruning method designed specifically for batched inference in LRMs, built on two components. First, we replace threshold-based selection with periodic top-k selection over the aggregated importance scores, which is unaffected by the shift that aggregation induces in the activation distribution, and which runs selection once per update period rather than at every token, preserving the speedup. Second, based on the observation that important neurons re-fire periodically during long reasoning generation, we introduce an activation memory that accumulates importance across update phases so that recurring neurons are retained.
  Experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our method outperforms the previous state-of-the-art adaptive pruning method by 39.7 percentage points in average accuracy at batch size 4 with 50% target sparsity on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, and reaches 1.40x speedup over dense inference at 50% actual sparsity.

</details>


### [88] [S2Dialog: Multimodal Dialogue Retrieval with Semantic and Acoustic-Style Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14029)
*Xueqi Wang,Zhigang Wang,Runqing Zhang,Zhenqi Jia,Junfeng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S2Dialog，一个用于多模态对话银行中对话级语义-风格检索的统一框架，通过对话级文本检索器、声学检索器以及对话级文本-声学对比学习，在DailyTalk数据集上取得了优异的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话检索对于情感识别、口语对话系统、对话式语音合成等任务至关重要，但现有方法局限于话语级或单模态匹配，难以捕捉整个对话的全局语义连贯性和风格一致性。

Method: S2Dialog由两个核心部分组成：(1) 对话级文本检索器，将对话的文本模态编码为对话级表示；(2) 对话级声学检索器，将对话的声学模态编码为对话级表示。此外，引入对话级文本-声学对比学习，将语义和风格相似的对话对齐，同时区分不相关的对话。

Result: 在多模态对话数据集DailyTalk上进行的大量实验表明，S2Dialog取得了突出的检索性能。

Conclusion: S2Dialog通过统一的对话级表示学习和跨模态对比学习，有效解决了多模态对话检索中全局语义连贯性和风格一致性问题，为对话级多模态检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal dialogue retrieval aims to retrieve dialogues from multimodal dialogue banks that are similar to a target dialogue in terms of both textual semantics and acoustic conversational styles. Such dialogue-level retrieval is crucial for many dialogue-related tasks, including Emotion Recognition in Conversation, Spoken Dialogue Systems, and Conversational Speech Synthesis, where external dialogue examples can provide valuable semantic and stylistic references. However, existing retrieval methods are still largely limited to utterance-level or unimodal matching, and often fail to capture the global semantic coherence and stylistic consistency of an entire dialogue. To address this gap, we propose S2Dialog, a unified framework for dialogue-level semantic-style retrieval from multimodal dialogue banks. Specifically, S2Dialog consists of a Dialogue-level Textual Retriever and a Dialogue-level Acoustic Retriever, which encode the textual and acoustic modalities of a dialogue into dialogue-level representations, respectively. To further enhance multimodal retrieval, we introduce Dialogue-level Textual-Acoustic Contrastive Learning, which aligns semantically and stylistically similar dialogues while distinguishing unrelated ones. Extensive experiments on the multimodal dialogue dataset DailyTalk demonstrate that S2Dialog achieves outstanding retrieval performance.

</details>


### [89] [HERMES: a multi-agent framework for structured knowledge extraction from ultra-long documents in geoscience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14055)
*Ziqi Song,Zongyuan Xiang,James G. Ogg,Bruce S. Lieberman,Gabi Ogg,Natalia López Carranza,Wen Du,Yufei Ye,Shuan Li,Zhong Peng,Shaoqi Yu,Juye Wei,Ying Zhou,Jieping Ye,Jiang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍HERMES，一个可扩展的多智能体框架，用于从超长科学文献中提取结构化数据。该系统应用于55卷《古无脊椎动物学论丛》，生成了包含32,277个化石分类学实体和451,878个属性的结构化数据库，实体与属性的平均F1分数分别约为0.90和0.91，相比全人工基线将每卷效率提升约六倍，且无需额外模型训练即可迁移至古地磁学和地球化学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球科学领域的权威科学知识大量封存于传统专著和历史文献中，非结构化的文本和复杂版式阻碍了计算化访问，亟需一种能够从超长科学文档中自动提取结构化数据的可扩展方法。

Method: 提出HERMES多智能体框架，由协调型大语言模型统一调度，将领域约束、验证规则和证据溯源整合到统一的文档级提取流程中，融合处理文本、表格、图表及图注等异构内容。

Result: 在55卷论丛上生成含32,277个分类实体和451,878个属性的数据库，跨化石类群提取性能稳定（实体F1约0.90、属性F1约0.91），每卷效率较全人工基线提升约六倍；在无需额外训练的情况下成功迁移至古地磁学和地球化学领域。

Conclusion: HERMES为将历史科学文献转化为FAIR导向的结构化数据提供了可行路径，为数据密集型学科和大规模知识整合构建了可持续的基础设施。

Abstract: Authoritative scientific knowledge in geoscience remains largely trapped in legacy monographs and historical literature, where unstructured text and complex layouts hinder computational access. We introduce HERMES, a scalable multi-agent framework that extracts structured data from ultra-long scientific documents. Using a coordinating large language model, HERMES integrates domain constraints, validation rules and evidence tracing within a unified document-level extraction process that incorporates parsed text, tables, figures and captions. Applied to the 55-volume Treatise on Invertebrate Paleontology, the system produced a structured database of 32,277 fossil taxonomic entities and 451,878 attributes, released online at https://treatise.geolex.org. Extraction performance remained stable across fossil groups (average F1 scores of approximately 0.90 for entities and 0.91 for attributes), improving per-volume efficiency approximately sixfold relative to the tested fully manual baseline. Evaluation in palaeomagnetism and geochemistry, conducted without additional model training, demonstrated transfer across distinct geoscience domains. This work provides a practical pathway to transform historical scientific literature into FAIR-oriented structured data, offering a sustainable infrastructure for data-intensive disciplines and large-scale knowledge integration.

</details>


### [90] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了"快速"与"慢速"硅基抽样（silicon sampling）的算法保真度，基于新加坡全国代表性调查数据，发现快速模式在计算效率和算法保真度上均优于慢速模式，但整体而言硅基抽样仍属早期方法，需谨慎使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅基抽样（使用LLM生成合成样本）有时能产生令人惊讶的好的人口估计，但尚未系统评估其执行速度（快速vs慢速模式）是否会影响估计的保真度。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为实践者提供选择不同抽样模式时的实证依据。

Method: 研究者以新加坡全国代表性调查受访者为基准，扩展并进行硅基抽样的实证评估，比较"快速"与"慢速"两种硅基抽样模式的算法保真度，评估其在人口均值估计、意见方差测量及潜在语境空间（latent contextual space）还原三方面的表现。

Result: 研究发现：硅基样本能产生中等保真度的人口均值估计，但持续低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在语境空间。在承认这些局限的前提下，"快速"模式在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于"慢速"模式。

Conclusion: 硅基抽样（即便使用当代前沿模型）仍处于早期发展阶段，只能在高谨慎度下使用。但若必须使用硅基抽样，"快速"模式是相对更优的选择，因为它不仅更高效，而且在算法保真度上系统性优于传统"慢速"模式。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Leading-Silence Augmentation and Multi-Stage Synthetic Supervision for the Second MLC-SLM Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14150)
*Kexin Shi,Renhe Sun,Yuge Huang,Ximeng Wang,Jiayi Zhou,Jian Liu,Malu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MLC-SLM挑战赛的参赛方案：任务1（说话人分离与识别）通过随机前导静音裁剪、时间戳一致性修正和EMA训练策略微调VibeVoice-ASR-7B，将tcpMER从18.30%降至16.73%；任务2（对话语音理解）通过构建合成问答对并微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct进行标签直接回答，将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛针对完整、未分段的 multilingual 对话，设置了说话人分离识别和对话理解两个任务，且评测时不提供 oracle 话语边界或说话人标签，任务2甚至无问答训练集，这对模型提出了更高要求，需要应对真实场景中的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 任务1采用VibeVoice-ASR-7B进行微调，引入随机前导静音裁剪以增强对对话起始边界的鲁棒性，结合一致的时间戳修正和指数移动平均（EMA）训练策略；任务2通过多模态候选生成、静音音频过滤和分布匹配增强构建合成问答对，微调Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct实现标签式直接回答。

Result: 任务1中，裁剪将tcpMER从18.30%降至17.27%，EMA进一步降至16.73%；任务2中，联合应用分布匹配增强和标签式直接回答将准确率从83.0%提升至86.0%。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练策略和合成数据增强方法在缺乏 oracle 标注和训练数据的条件下，有效地提升了多语言对话语音处理任务（说话人识别与对话理解）的性能，验证了数据增强与训练技巧在受限场景下的实用价值。

Abstract: The second Multilingual Conversational Speech Language Model (MLC-SLM) Challenge evaluates two tasks over complete, unsegmented multilingual conversations: speaker diarization and recognition (Task 1) and conversational speech understanding (Task 2). Neither task provides oracle utterance boundaries or speaker labels at evaluation, and Task 2 provides no question-answer training set. For Task 1, we fine-tune VibeVoice-ASR-7B with random leading-silence cropping, consistent timestamp correction, and an exponential moving average (EMA) training strategy. For Task 2, we construct synthetic question-answer pairs through multimodal candidate generation, silent-audio filtering, and distribution-matched augmentation, and fine-tune Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct for tagged direct answering. On the Task 1 evaluation set, cropping reduces tcpMER from 18.30% to 17.27%, and EMA further reduces it to 16.73%. On the Task 2 evaluation set, jointly applying distribution-matched augmentation and tagged direct answering raises accuracy from 83.0% to 86.0%.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Do Legal RAG Systems Still Hallucinate?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14210)
*Souvick Das,Sallam Abualhaija,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对法律领域的八个检索增强生成（RAG）系统进行了细粒度的幻觉行为分析，覆盖两个法律语料库（英文GDPR和法国民法典），采用声明级和答案级评估，发现幻觉问题仍然普遍存在，最佳系统仅有不到10%的响应存在幻觉，而最差系统接近50%；同时发现包含错误前提的问题在人工撰写的测试集上产生极高的幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律领域的RAG系统中，幻觉（未基于检索内容生成的回答）可能导致严重后果，但此前缺乏对该问题的系统化、细粒度理解。作者旨在通过多系统、多语料库、多评估维度的综合分析，揭示法律RAG系统中幻觉行为的模式与严重程度。

Method: 作者对八个法律RAG系统在两个法律语料库（英文GDPR、法国民法典）上进行幻觉行为的细粒度分析。采用声明级（claim-level）和答案级（answer-level）两种评估方式，报告幻觉密度和严重程度，并按照问题类别和用户角色进行分析。此外，使用142个由法律专家撰写的问题作为独立验证集。

Result: 幻觉问题在测试的所有系统中普遍存在：最佳系统的响应中幻觉占比不到10%，而最差系统接近一半的响应存在幻觉。在人工撰写的测试集上，包含错误前提（需要被拒绝的假设）的问题产生了最高的幻觉率。

Conclusion: 法律领域的RAG系统幻觉问题仍然严峻，尤其是面对含有错误前提的问题时表现最差，这提示未来需要更关注对错误前提的识别与拒绝能力，以提升法律RAG系统的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Hallucination is a major challenge for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems in the legal domain, where ungrounded answers can lead to serious consequences. To better understand this problem, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of hallucination behavior in eight legal RAG systems across two legal corpora, the GDPR (in English) and a national civil law (in French). Using claim-level and answer-level evaluation, we report on hallucination density and severity, analyze performance across question categories and user personas, and validate our findings on an independent set of 142 legal-expert-authored questions. Our results show that hallucinations remain pervasive, ranging from less than 10% of responses for the best-performing systems to nearly half in the worst case. We further find that false-premise questions, containing incorrect assumptions that must be rejected, produce high hallucination rates on the manually-drafted questions.

</details>


### [93] [The More Popular, The Harder to Forget: Adaptive Popularity for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14229)
*Anna Borisiuk,Andrey Savchenko,Alexander Panchenko,Elena Tutubalina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaPop（自适应流行度）方法，结合局部token置信度与基于数据流行度的指数项，并通过双上升控制器自动调节遗忘-保留平衡，使LLM遗忘时对高频事实施加更多梯度压力，漏泄量比现有方法降低约5倍（释义查询）和1.6倍（对抗改写）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法对所有训练数据施加统一梯度压力，忽视了高频事实在预训练中被记忆更深、更难被移除的特性，导致遗忘不充分或保留能力受损。

Method: AdaPop将局部token置信度与按事实流行度变化的指数项结合（流行度来自外部代理如Wikidata链接数或LLM裁判），并通过双上升控制器（dual-ascent）逐epoch自动调节保留惩罚权重，以自适应地平衡遗忘与保留。

Result: 在三个模型家族和两个基准上，AdaPop在释义查询下漏泄量约为对比方法的1/5（少约5倍），在对抗改写下约为1.6倍更少；内部指标显示遗忘集隐藏状态离预遗忘模型更远，而保留集表征保持接近。

Conclusion: 按事实流行度自适应调整遗忘压力能显著提升LLM遗忘的鲁棒性与保留能力，验证了流行度感知的遗忘策略有效性。

Abstract: Popular facts are memorised more deeply during pretraining and resist removal longer than rare ones, yet existing LLM unlearning methods apply uniform gradient pressure regardless of training-data frequency. We propose the AdaPop (Adaptive Popularity) method, which combines local token confidence with a per-fact popularity-dependent exponent derived from an external proxy (e.g., Wikidata sitelinks, LLM-as-Judge), and automates the forget-retain balance via a dual-ascent controller that adjusts the retain penalty each epoch. Across three model families and two benchmarks, AdaPop leaks ~5x less forgotten content than competing methods under paraphrased queries and ~1.6x less under adversarial reformulations. We support our analysis with internal metrics: under our method, forget-set hidden states move further from the pre-unlearning model's states than under other methods, while retain-set representations remain close.

</details>


### [94] [SimpleOPD: Simple Tokenizer-Agnostic On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14277)
*Haonan He,Haodi Lei,Yun Luo,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Yizhuo Li,Shengji Tang,Zhilin Wang,Runzhe Zhan,Lei Bai,Ganqu Cui,Fangchen Yu,Yafu Li,Peng Ye,Ning Ding,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将长上下文推理教师模型（SU-01）的证明推理能力蒸馏到短上下文学生模型中，通过在共享文本空间进行On-policy蒸馏、引入学生参考KL损失、掩盖终止token优势等方法，解决了tokenizer不匹配、分布不匹配、长度爆炸和训练不稳定等问题，并在多个学生模型上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏（OPD）是传递教师模型推理能力的有效方法，但从长上下文推理教师向短上下文学生迁移时面临tokenizer不匹配、教师-学生分布不匹配、响应长度爆炸和训练不稳定等实际挑战，需要针对性地解决这些问题。

Method: 1）在共享文本空间执行OPD，仅对齐在师生tokenizer下占据相同文本跨度的token；2）引入学生参考KL损失约束学生不偏离初始策略过远；3）对特殊终止token（如</think>和<|im_end|>）的优势进行掩码处理，缓解过长生成和频繁截断问题。

Result: 在Qwen3、Qwen3.5、Intern-S2、GLM-4.7、Gemma-4等多个同族和异族学生模型上取得一致的数学推理提升。Intern-S2-Preview在ProofBench上提升21.2分达到55.2，超越Gemini-2.5-Pro；在HLE和HiPhO等科学基准上也取得提升，表明OPD迁移的推理能力可泛化到数学训练域之外。

Conclusion: 通过共享文本空间对齐、参考KL损失和终止token优势掩码这三项策略，能够有效解决跨tokenizer的长上下文推理蒸馏中的关键挑战，实现稳定且可泛化的推理能力迁移。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers a promising way to transfer reasoning capabilities from stronger teacher models, but applying it to long-context reasoning teachers and short-context students introduces practical challenges, including tokenizer mismatch, teacher-student distribution mismatch, response length explosion, and training instability. In this work, we study this setting by transferring proof-reasoning capabilities from the long-context reasoning model SU-01 to short-context student models. To handle tokenizer differences, we perform OPD in a shared text space and align only tokens that occupy identical text spans under the student and teacher tokenizers. To mitigate the problem of excessive generation length and frequent truncation, we introduce a student reference KL loss and mask the advantages of special termination tokens such as </think> and <|im_end|>. This strategy constrains the student from drifting excessively from its initial policy, thereby mitigating the teacher-student distribution mismatch problem and fostering steady length growth. Experiments on both same-family and different-family student models, including Qwen3, Qwen3.5, Intern-S2, GLM-4.7, Gemma-4, show consistent gains in mathematical reasoning, especially natural-language math proving. Notably, Intern-S2-Preview improves by 21.2 points on ProofBench, reaching 55.2 and surpassing Gemini-2.5-Pro. It also improves on science benchmarks such as HLE and HiPhO, suggesting that OPD transfers reasoning capabilities that generalize beyond the mathematical training domain.

</details>


### [95] [Envs-FORGE: Frontier-Optimized Reward-Grounded Environment Synthesis for Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14312)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Zhichao Shi,Hao Zhou,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Envs-FORGE，一种将验证器奖励转化为逐种子环境合成动作的提示策略，通过 MILP 选择难度与方向，在多个 benchmark 上显著超越固定配方基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定提示策略（如 few-shot、Self-Instruct、Evol-Instruct）对所有种子数据应用相同的提示策略，无法根据当前策略的学习状态自适应调整任务难度和方向，导致训练环境缺乏可靠的难度梯度和奖励信号。

Method: Envs-FORGE 将验证器奖励转换为逐种子的环境合成动作：先估计种子数据的通过率，在目标学习前沿周围评估六个投影方向的动作，然后通过每个种子的混合整数线性规划（MILP）选择最合适动作来指导生成。所选动作驱动指令、配件、oracle 解、测试和 Docker 环境的同步重写，只有经过黄金验证的样本才进入 RL 训练。MILP 形式还支持可选的软技能覆盖用于组合规划。

Result: 在 Qwen 3.5 35B 上，Envs-FORGE 在 tb-core 上相比 Base 提高 Pass@1 9.2 个百分点（40.0%→49.2%），在 tb-2.0 上提高 6.4 个百分点（23.0%→29.4%），分别超出最强固定配方基线 2.4 和 2.1 个百分点。SWE-bench Verified 达 77.1%（Base 为 73.4%），并在 4B-35B 规模模型上提升 tb-core 6.8-9.2 点。所有方法均导出 100 个验证环境，消耗 2.27M-2.88M 合成 tokens，保证同口径对比。

Conclusion: 自适应的逐种子环境合成策略相比固定配方能更有效地为终端 RL agent 提供合适的训练难度与奖励信号，在相同训练集规模和操作成本下取得一致且显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for terminal agents needs executable training environments with reliable rewards and useful difficulty. Fixed recipes such as few-shot, Self-Instruct, and Evol-Instruct apply the same prompting policy to every seed, even when the current policy would benefit from a harder, easier, or simply different task. We present Envs-FORGE, a prompting policy that converts verifier rewards into per-seed environment-synthesis actions. Envs-FORGE estimates seed pass rates, scores six projection--direction actions around a target learning frontier, and solves a per-seed mixed-integer linear program (MILP) to choose the action that conditions generation. The selected action drives synchronized rewriting of the instruction, fixtures, oracle solution, tests, and Docker environment; only gold-verified bundles enter RL training. The indexed MILP form also supports optional soft skill coverage for portfolio planning. On Qwen 3.5 35B, Envs-FORGE improves Pass@1 over Base by 9.2 percentage points on tb-core (40.0% to 49.2%) and 6.4 points on tb-2.0 (23.0% to 29.4%), exceeding the strongest fixed-recipe baseline by 2.4 and 2.1 points. It reaches 77.1% on SWE-bench Verified versus 73.4% for Base, and improves tb-core by 6.8--9.2 points across the evaluated 4B--35B models. All synthesis methods export 100 verified environments and use 2.27M--2.88M synthesis tokens, placing the comparison at the same downstream training-set size and the same operational scale. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/DataArc-SynData-Toolkit/.

</details>


### [96] [Local and Global Regimes of Geometric Complexity in Language Model Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14361)
*Arwa Osman,Marco Baroni,Iuri Macocco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了词汇多样性（数据集中唯一末词项的数量）如何影响语言模型数据集的固有维度（ID）估计。作者发现存在一个与尺度相关的两阶段转变：在低词汇多样性条件下，较少唯一词的样本产生更高的ID；而在高词汇多样性条件下，顺序反转，更多唯一词的样本产生更高的ID。作者推导出了一个精确的、无参数的公式来预测该反转点，该公式在所有测试尺度下均与观测到的转变点吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固有维度（ID）被广泛用于探测语言模型的表征复杂度，但ID差异究竟是反映了语言本身的属性，还是数据集构造过程中的人为假象，这一问题尚不明确。作者希望厘清词汇多样性这一数据构造因素对ID估计的具体影响，以便正确解读ID作为表征复杂度指标的含义。

Method: 作者聚焦于词汇多样性（数据集中唯一末词项的数量）对ID估计的影响，通过系统性的尺度实验，在不同词汇多样性条件下测量ID，并据此推导出一个精确且无参数的公式来描述ID反转点的位置，并通过实验数据验证该公式在多个尺度下的准确性。

Result: 发现了一个尺度依赖的两阶段转变：低词汇多样性区间内，唯一词较少的条件产生更高的ID；高词汇多样性区间内，顺序反转，唯一词更多的条件产生更高的ID。推导出的无参数反转点公式在所有测试尺度下均与观测结果精确匹配。

Conclusion: 一方面，研究警示在将表征集合的固有维度解读为复杂度线索时需格外谨慎，因为ID可能受数据集构造方式的影响；另一方面，两个ID阶段的发现揭示了LLM中语言数据组织的一般性原则，为理解其内部流形结构提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Intrinsic dimensionality (ID) is widely used to probe the representational complexity of language models, but it remains unclear whether ID differences reflect properties of language itself or artefacts of how the underlying dataset was constructed. In this paper, we focus specifically on how lexical diversity, the number of unique last-token items present in a dataset, affects ID estimates of that dataset. We find a scale-dependent transition between two regimes: at low lexical diversity, conditions with fewer unique final words produce higher ID, while at high lexical diversity, this ordering reverses, and conditions with more unique words produce higher ID. We derive an exact, parameter-free formula for the point at which this reversal occurs, which matches the observed transition point at every scale tested. On the one hand, our results highlight how care must be taken when interpreting the intrinsic dimensionality of a set of representations as a straightforward cue of their complexity. On the other hand, our discovery of the two ID regimes reveals a general principle of organisation of linguistic data in LLMs that sheds new light on their inner manifold structures.

</details>


### [97] [A Survey of Large Models in Sports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14377)
*Yichen Xu,Jianzhe Ma,Chuhan Wang,Zhonghao Cao,Liangyu Chen,Wenxuan Wang,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于大型模型（尤其是多模态大语言模型）在体育领域应用的综合性综述，涵盖任务与应用分类、数据集与基准分析、以及当前挑战与未来方向的批判性讨论，旨在推动大型模型驱动的体育智能化研究与实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体育在全球范围内受到越来越多的关注，而（多模态）大型语言模型的快速发展为解决体育理解、分析与交互中的复杂问题提供了变革性潜力。然而，目前缺乏一个系统性的综述来梳理大型模型在体育领域的应用全貌，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 作者采用综述方法论，从三个维度展开：(1) 按不同参与者群体梳理体育领域中的任务与应用；(2) 系统分析体育相关的数据集与基准；(3) 对当前挑战与未来研究方向进行批判性讨论。

Result: 本文提供了大型模型在体育领域的系统性综述框架，涵盖任务/应用概述、数据集/基准分析以及挑战与展望，并维护了一个开源GitHub仓库以持续更新相关资源。

Conclusion: 该综述为大型模型驱动的体育智能研究与实践奠定了坚实基础，明确了该领域的现状、差距与未来发展方向，对推动跨学科研究具有重要意义。

Abstract: Sports have witnessed growing global enthusiasm in recent years, serving as a vital force for physical health, cultural exchange, social connection, and economic growth. The rapid advancement of large models, particularly (multimodal) large language models (M)LLMs, has demonstrated transformative potential to reshape sports understanding, analysis, and interaction across diverse domains. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of large models in sports, including (i) an overview of tasks and applications across different participant groups; (ii) a detailed analysis of sports-related datasets and benchmarks; and (iii) a critical discussion of current challenges and future directions. Our goal is to establish a foundation for advancing research and practical development of large-model-driven sports intelligence. An open-source GitHub repository is maintained at: https://github.com/Road2Redemption/Awesome_Large_Models_In_Sports1.

</details>


### [98] [Information Satisfaction: A Reader-Centered Axis for Summarization Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14457)
*Isabel Cachola,William Walden,Reno Kriz,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了摘要评估指标在衡量摘要对个体用户实用性（信息满意度）方面的不足，发现包括LLM-as-judge在内的主流指标对信息内容扰动测试不敏感，且与传统及基于LLM的指标相比，与专家人类判断的一致性很差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的摘要评估指标（如ROUGE、BERTScore等）主要关注一般性摘要质量或特定属性（如可读性、事实性），但无法衡量摘要对个体用户的实用性。查询聚焦摘要虽然部分解决了这一问题，但用户的单一短查询往往不足以区分不同背景（如研究人员与医生的不同需求）的信息需求。而用户的背景或人设（角色和专业能力）相对稳定，可以作为评估摘要是否满足读者需求的实用信号。

Method: 作者首先评估了主流摘要评估指标（包括强LLM-as-judge指标）对信息差异和人设差异的敏感程度，设计扰动测试来验证这些指标。随后进行了专家人类评估，基于特定个人背景和使用场景，衡量专家对摘要信息满意度的偏好判断。

Result: 发现许多主流指标（包括强LLM-as-judge指标）在信息内容的基本扰动测试中失败；传统指标和基于LLM的指标都不足以衡量信息满意度，与人类判断的一致性很低。

Conclusion: 当前的摘要评估方法（无论是传统还是基于LLM的）均无法有效衡量摘要对个体用户的实际信息满足程度，需要开发能捕捉用户人设/背景信息需求的新的评估范式。

Abstract: The majority of work on summarization evaluation focuses on general summary quality (e.g., ROUGE, BERTScore) or specific desired properties (e.g., readability, factuality). However, these metrics fail to measure the utility of a summary to an individual user. For example, a biomedical researcher learning about the latest vaccine research will have different informational needs from a family doctor. Query-focused summarization captures part of this need, but in practice, users rarely state everything relevant in a query: a single short query is likely inadequate to distinguish the needs of a researcher from those of a physician. By contrast, a reader's background or persona (their role and expertise) is comparatively stable across queries and recovers much of this missing context, which makes it a practical signal for assessing whether a summary satisfies that reader's needs. In this work, we assess how sensitive popular summarization metrics are to both informational and persona differences, and find that many popular metrics, including strong LLM-as-judge metrics, fail basic perturbation tests of informational content. We additionally conduct an expert human evaluation, measuring summary preferences based on information satisfaction given a specific person's background and use case. We find that both traditional and LLM-based metrics are insufficient measures of information satisfaction and agree poorly with human judgment.

</details>


### [99] [You Only Pass Once: Answering and Abstaining Together in a Single Forward Pass of a Frozen Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14465)
*Ziyang Luo,Zhongyao Chu,Xinjie He,Youting Wang,Xukui Qin,Runxiong Wu,Yan-Syuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: YOPO（You Only Pass Once）系统在冻结的Qwen2.5骨干网络上，通过条件引导探针（steering probe）写入残差流恢复推理能力，同时利用零样本充分性方向（zero-shot sufficiency direction）读取残差流实现弃权（abstain）机制，两者在一次前向传播中同时运行。为解决两者干扰问题，作者训练一个小型网络从引导后的残差重建引导前的残差，在无弃权标签的情况下实现单次前向传播的"回答-引导-弃权"三位一体能力。实验显示端到端三路准确率较冻结基线翻倍以上（1.5B alphaNLI从0.375提升至0.798），单次前向传播在十种骨干网络和六种模型家族上均优于两次前向传播的基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型在推理任务上有两个相互耦合的弱点：一是未充分利用其残差流中已编码的证据信息，二是无法检测输入信息是否充分，导致产生幻觉式回答（confabulation）。现有研究分别针对这两个问题（条件引导探针与充分性方向），但两者部署在同一残差流上时会产生干扰——引导写入会改变充分性方向所读取的状态，若分开两次前向传播则会翻倍推理成本。本文旨在解决这一干扰问题，实现单次前向传播下的推理、引导与弃权一体化。

Method: 作者提出YOPO系统，核心设计是：保持充分性方向（sufficiency direction）固定不变，训练一个小型神经网络重建网络，以（引导后残差, 干净残差）对均方误差为损失函数（不使用弃权标签），从引导后的残差流重建引导前的残差，然后在重建残差上读取充分性方向进行弃权判断。这样既保留引导写入对推理准确率的增益，又克服了引导写入对弃权读取状态的干扰，实现一次前向传播中同时完成回答、引导与弃权。实验在Qwen2.5的1.5B/3B/7B规模以及多模型家族迁移测试上验证。

Result: YOPO在三路（回答、引导、弃权）端到端准确率上较冻结基线提升超过一倍（1.5B alphaNLI从0.375增至0.798）；在三个规模上均优于两次前向传播的参照方法（0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863）；在六个模型家族共十种骨干网络上得到验证。作者还绘制了容量-迁移前沿（capacity-transfer frontier），量化了"弃权不应被训练进去"的原则。通过源端审计发现alphaNLI构造中泄漏的表面伪影，因此在本地标签复制（SQuAD2、RepLiQA、MuSiQue）上锚定架构声明。在标准四域套件上贡献了据作者所知首个answer-or-abstain基准，其门控在域内数据集上全面最优，标签无关方向是唯一能在域迁移中存活的栅族。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在冻结骨干网络上，通过固定充分性方向并用重建网络解耦引导写入与弃权读取之间的干扰，可以在单次前向传播中同时实现推理增强与信息充分性弃权，且性能超越两次前向传播的基准。此外，论文强调"弃权不应被训练进来"的原则，并通过严谨的源端审计（发现并规避alphaNLI标签泄漏）以及本地标签数据集上的验证，确保了架构声明的可信度，同时提供了首个answer-or-abstain四域基准供社区使用。

Abstract: A frozen language model on reasoning tasks has two coupled weaknesses: it under-uses evidence its own residual stream already encodes, and it fails to detect when the input is insufficient to answer, so it confabulates. This paper consolidates two research lines that address these on the same residual stream: a conditional steering probe writes the stream at mid-stack layers and recovers reasoning accuracy from a frozen backbone, and a zero-shot sufficiency direction reads the stream and abstains when information is insufficient. Deployed in one forward pass they interfere: the steering write shifts the state the direction reads, costing up to 8 AUROC points of cross-domain transfer on small models; a separate clean pass doubles inference cost. We keep the direction fixed and train a small network to reconstruct the pre-steering residual from the steered one -- mean-squared error on (steered, clean) pairs, no sufficiency labels -- and read the direction on the reconstruction. The resulting system, YOPO (You Only Pass Once), answers, steers, and abstains in one forward pass of a frozen Qwen2.5 backbone (1.5B/3B/7B). End to end, three-way accuracy more than doubles the frozen baseline (0.375->0.798 on 1.5B alphaNLI) and one pass beats the two-pass reference at every scale (0.798/0.830/0.893 vs 0.753/0.790/0.863) and on ten backbones across six model families. We chart the capacity-transfer frontier quantifying the principle that abstention should not be trained in; a source-side audit catches our own alphaNLI construction leaking a surface artifact, so architectural claims are anchored on native-label replications (SQuAD2, RepLiQA, MuSiQue); and on the standard four-domain suite we contribute, to our knowledge, the first answer-or-abstain benchmark, where our gate tops every in-domain dataset and the label-free direction is the only gate family to survive domain transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricForge框架，通过反射式进化从少量带环境奖励标注的轨迹中自动生成可读的评判规则文本，用于语言模型评测智能体。实验表明其原始一致性虽未显著优于G-Eval，但将虚假通过率（false-pass rate）降低近一半，更忠实地评估轨迹结果，是奖励-free场景下更可靠的评判器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体时，常需用另一个LM作为自动评判器（judge），因为真实环境奖励昂贵、缓慢或不可用。现有评判器要么手写评分规则（如G-Eval），要么微调评判权重，但两者都倾向于给流畅但不成功的轨迹以高分。作者希望将评判规则植根于真实的ground-truth结果，提高评判的可信度。

Method: RubricForge通过反射式进化（reflective evolution）在少量带标签轨迹上迭代优化评判规则文本，目标是最大化与环境奖励的一致性，然后冻结该规则，在无需环境访问的单次模型调用中应用于留出轨迹。规则以人类可读文本形式存在，每条判定可归因到具体命名标准。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的7B模型作为智能体和评判器，在tau-bench（173条标注轨迹）和WebShop（160条）上进行实验：与G-Eval相比，总体一致性差异不显著（McNemar p=0.248），绝对分数校准略偏向通用评判器（|err|差-0.048, p=2x10^-4）。但RubricForge将虚假通过率从0.173降至0.115（tau-bench），并更忠实地排序WebShop结果（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 对于无奖励（reward-free）评估器，虚假通过率而非总体一致性才是部署相关的关键指标，因为虚假通过会发布故障智能体，而虚假失败仅需重试。RubricForge在降低虚假通过率方面显著更优，证明了以ground-truth为锚的规则进化方向的有效性。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time.
